Shedinja (+9 Stat Points, +Composure Talent, +Observation Adept, +30ft. hovering, +30ft. phasing, -10ft. walking, HP Class Weak -> Pitiful, Learns Shadow Claw, Type Bug / Ground -> Bug / Ghost, Ability Becomes Wonder Guard)
Special Note: when Nincada evolves into Ninjask, then a Shedinja will also be created from the husk of the Ninjask. When evolving, create an exact copy of the Nincada sheet, keeping all held items onto the first sheet but not the second- the original sheet is the Pokémon that evolves into Ninjask, and the copied sheet is the one that evolves into Shedinja, becoming separate Pokémon. The Shedinja is considered your Pokémon but still must be caught or befriended upon evolution. Due to the strange way Shedinja works that could potentially make it borderline useless in a TTRPG setting, Shedinja has been altered slightly- upon evolution, the Shedinja’s HP is set to 1, and it gains +1 HP for every 3 levels it has reached. Additionally, Shedinja has its own HP class, Pitiful, which allows it to gain 1d2 HP per level up.

Special Note: In order to cause Kyogre, Groudon, or Rayquaza to undergo Primal Reversion or Mega Evolution, their trainer (/the Pokémon itself when applicable, such as for Pokémon Mystery Dungeon) must take an action (/full turn) to cause them to undergo their respective transformation. Additionally, Kyogre and Groudon MUST be holding the Blue Orb and Red Orb respectively to undergo Primal Reversion, and Rayquaza MUST have Dragon Ascent in its current learned moves in order to undergo Mega Evolution. Only one of these Pokémon can only undergo their special transformations on a single team, so if Kyogre undergoes Primal Reversion, the same trainer cannot have a Groudon undergo Primal Reversion or Mega Evolve their Rayquaza.

Species: Rotom 
Type: Electric / Ghost
Size: Small
Weight: Feather
HP: 20
HP Class: Average
Attack: 5
Defense: 7
Special Attack: 9
Special Defense: 7
Speed: 9
Movement: 50ft. phasing, 30ft. hovering
Abilities: Levitate
Talents: (Adept) Tech, Observation, Style
Diet & Habitat: No Diet or Herbivore / Urban, Forests
Egg Group: Amorphous
Evolution: Does Not Evolve
Special Note: By using a Rotom Catalog, Rotom can enter into any of its appliance forms during a Downtime- Heat, Wash, Frost, Fan, and Mow. Each has their own separate statblock, using Rotom’s moveset, nature, and level. Rotom Heat automatically learns Heat Wave, Rotom Wash automatically learns Hydro Pump, Rotom Frost automatically learns Blizzard, Rotom Fan automatically learns Air Slash, and Rotom Mow automatically learns Leaf Storm. Whenever Rotom gains EXP and levels up, the level and stat points are distributed individually across all of Rotom’s formes.

Whenever Kyurem or a fused Kyurem gains EXP, so does its counterpart, and they share the same nature as well- however, the Reshiram or Zekrom it is fused with does not gain EXP, only Kyurem.

Special Note: Aegislash Blade and Shield Forme can have stat points independent of one another. Upon a Doublade evolving into Aegislash, its base stats in Blade forme are what it has upon evolving, and its base stats in Shield forme are what its stats are after swapping its Attack with its Defense and its Special Attack with its Special Defense. When leveling up on odd/even levels, you put +2/+1 stat points into both Blade Forme and Shield Forme stats. On Honedge’s sheet, an example of what this will look like for Aegislash has been provided in blue. This split in stats only occurs for Aegislash, not for Honedge or Doublade.

Species: 50% Zygarde 
Type: Dragon / Ground
Size: Huge
Weight: Massive
HP: 22
HP Class: Bulky

Defense: 12
Special Attack: 8
Special Defense: 9
Speed: 9
Movement: 70ft. walking
Abilities: Aura Break, Power Construct
Talents: (Adept) Nature, (Adept) Observation, (Adept) Insight, Athletics, Force
Diet & Habitat: Omnivore / Habitat Unknown
Egg Group: No Eggs Discovered
Evolution: Does Not Evolve
Special Note: When leveling up Zygarde, you individually level up each one of Zygarde’s formes- 10% forme, 50% forme, and Complete Forme. While your GM may rule it differently if they so wish, all sheets of the individual Zygarde gain the same stats, and they share the same EXP, learnset and learned moves, PP, and statistics that aren’t forme dependent. For example, if your Zygarde levels up while in 50% forme, and you add +1 point to its Attack, both its 10% forme and Complete forme gain +1 Attack as well, and that is the level up for all formes. However, HP is rolled individually for all Zygarde formes due to different HP Classes. Whenever Zygarde 10% or 50% would change to Complete forme as a result of Power Construct, HP percentage and PP used are carried over to Complete forme (ex. if a Zygarde 10%/50% has 150/245 max HP and transforms into Zygarde Complete at half of its max HP, then Zygarde would go from 150/245 max HP to 300/490 max HP with 150/245 current HP)

Special Note: When leveling up Zygarde, you individually level up each one of Zygarde’s formes- 10% forme, 50% forme, and Complete Forme. While your GM may rule it differently if they so wish, all sheets of the individual Zygarde gain the same stats, and they share the same EXP, learnset and learned moves, PP, and statistics that aren’t forme dependent. For example, if your Zygarde levels up while in 50% forme, and you add +1 point to its Attack, both its 10% forme and Complete forme gain +1 Attack as well, and that is the level up for all formes. However, HP is rolled individually for all Zygarde formes due to different HP Classes. Whenever Zygarde 10% or 50% would change to Complete forme as a result of Power Construct, HP percentage and PP used are carried over to Complete forme (ex. if a Zygarde 10%/50% has 150/245 max HP and transforms into Zygarde Complete at half of its max HP, then Zygarde would go from 150/245 max HP to 300/490 max HP with 150/245 current HP)

Special Note: When Toxel evolves, it will evolve to a different Toxtricity depending on its nature. If the Toxel’s nature is Hardy, Brave, Adamant, Naughty, Docile, Impish, Lax, Hasty, Jolly, Naive, Rash, Sassy, or Quirky, then it will evolve into Amped forme. If the Toxel’s nature is Lonely, Bold, Relaxed, Timid, Serious, Modest, Mild, Quiet, Bashful, Calm, Gentle, or Careful, then it will evolve into Low Key forme. Additionally, when evolving, Amped forme will have Static become Plus, and Low Key forme will have Static become Minus.

Species: Milcery
Type: Fairy
Size: Small
Weight: Feather
HP: 20
HP Class: Average
Attack: 4
Defense: 4
Special Attack: 5
Special Defense: 6
Speed: 3
Movement: 25ft. hovering, 10ft. walking
Abilities: Sweet Veil, Sugar Rush
Talents: Vitality
Diet & Habitat: Herbivore / Urban, Forests
Egg Group: Fairy, Amorphous
Evolution: Reach Level 25 + Refer To Special Note
Alcremie (+9 Stat Points, +Recovery Talent, +Vitality Adept, +20ft. walking, -25ft. hovering, Learns Decorate)
Special Note: Milcery evolves into a unique colored Alcremie based on a myriad of factors (only its appearance changes- nothing about its moves or stats changes whatsoever). Milcery’s appearance on evolution is dependent on 3 different factors - Which Sweet its holding (Strawberry Sweet, Berry Sweet, Love Sweet, Star Sweet, Clover Sweet, Flower Sweet, Ribbon Sweet) - What time of day it is (Day [Vanilla Cream, Ruby Cream, Ruby Swirl, Caramel Swirl], Night [Matcha Cream, Mint Cream, Lemon Cream, Salted Cream], or Evening [Rainbow Swirl]) - What berry the Milcery eats (A healing berry [Vanilla Cream, Caramel Swirl], a status cure berry [Ruby Cream, Ruby Swirl], a friendship berry [Matcha Cream, Lemon Cream], a type resist berry [Mint Cream, Salted Cream], or a berry with no effects [Rainbow Swirl]) For example, in order to get a Ruby Cream Alcremie, you must feed a Milcery a status cure berry, such as a Cheri or Pecha berry, during the day, upon which it will instantly evolve into Alcremie, and in order to get a Matcha Cream Alcremie, you must feed a Milcery a friendship berry during the night. A Milcery must be level 25 at minimum before it becomes able to evolve by these means.

Evolution: Does Not Evolve
Special Note: By using the Reins of Unity with both Calyrex and either Glastrier or Spectrier captured or befriended, they can be unified into Ice Rider Calyrex or Shadow Rider Calyrex. Each has their own separate statblock, combining the movesets of Calyrex and the mounted partner into a single joint moveset of your choosing (the 4 move limit still applies). Ice Rider Calyrex automatically learns Glacial Lance, and Shadow Rider Calyrex automatically learns Astral Barrage. Whenever Calyrex or a mounted Calyrex gains EXP, so does its counterpart, and they share the same nature as well- however, the Glastrier or Spectrier it is mounted with does not gain EXP, only Calyrex.
